% 7.1.1
% The introductory mantra:
07*0001_01 nārāyaṇaṁ namaskr̥tya naraṁ caiva narottamam
07*0001_02 devīṁ sarasvatīṁ caiva tato jayam udīrayet
% Before the introductory mantra, Ś1 ins.: oṁ
% svasti | śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ | oṁ namo bhagavate śrīvāsudevāya |
% śrīḥ; K1 oṁ svasti | oṁ namaḥ śrīgaṇeśāya | oṁ namaḥ
% kr̥ṣṇāya | oṁ namo vighnahartre | oṁ; K2 oṁ; K3 oṁ śrī-
% gaṇeśāya namaḥ | śrīkr̥ṣṇāya namaḥ | oṁ; K5 oṁ namaḥ śrī-
% gaṇeśāya | sarasvatyai namaḥ |; B1 oṁ namo bhagavate vāsu-
% devāya |; B2 oṁ namo gaṇapataye | oṁ namo bhagavate vāsu-
% devāya |; B3 śrīdurgā | oṁ namo bhagavate vāsudevāya |; B4
% oṁ gaṇeśāya namaḥ |; B5 śrī namo bhagavate vāsudevāya | oṁ;
% Dc1 śrīgurugopālagaurīgaṇapatibhyaḥ |; Dn2 śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ |
07*0002_01 jayati parāśarasūnuḥ satyavatīhr̥dayanandano vyāsaḥ
07*0002_02 yasyāsyakamalagalitaṁ vāṅmayam amr̥taṁ jagat pibati

% cont.:

07_001_0003 ehy ehi bho spardhase tvaṁ mayā sārdhaṁ hi sūtaja

07_008_0049 sa śatrubhiḥ parivr̥to bahudhā raṇamūrdhani
07_008_0050 vyasyan bāṇasahasrāṇi yodheṣu ca gajeṣu ca
07_008_0051 sa karma duṣkaraṁ kr̥tvā saṁgrāme śatrutāpanaḥ
07_008_0052 śatrubhir nihataḥ saṁkhye pr̥tanāyāṁ yudhiṣṭhira
07_008_0053 sa rājā pretakr̥tyāni tasya kr̥tvā śucānvitaḥ
07_008_0054 śocann ahani rātrau ca nālabhat sukham ātmanaḥ
07_008_0055 tasya śokaṁ viditvā tu putravyasanasaṁbhavam
07_008_0056 athājagāma devarṣir nārado ’sya samīpataḥ
07_008_0057 sa tu rājā mahābhāgo dr̥ṣṭvā devarṣisattamam
07_008_0058 pūjayitvā yathānyāyaṁ kathām akathayat tadā
07_008_0059 tasya sarvaṁ samācaṣṭa yathā vr̥ttaṁ nareśvara
07_008_0060 śatrubhir vijayaṁ saṁkhye putrasya ca vadhaṁ tathā
07_008_0061 mama putro mahāvīrya indraviṣṇusamadyutiḥ
07_008_0062 śatrubhir bahubhiḥ saṁkhye parākramya hato balī
07_008_0063 ka eṣa mr̥tyur bhagavan kiṁvīryabalapauruṣaḥ
07_008_0064 etad icchāmi tattvena śrotuṁ matimatāṁ vara

07_008=0066 nārada uvāca
07_008_0067 śr̥ṇu rājan mahābāho ākhyānaṁ bahuvistaram

07_008_0070 asaṁhr̥taṁ mahātejā dr̥ṣṭvā jagad idaṁ prabhuḥ
07_008_0071 tasya cintā samutpannā saṁhāraṁ prati pārthiva

07_008_0075 tato divaṁ bhuvaṁ khaṁ ca jvālāmālāsamāvr̥tam
07_008_0076 carācaraṁ jagat sarvaṁ dadāha bhagavān prabhuḥ
07_008_0077 tato hatāni bhūtāni carāṇi sthāvarāṇi ca
07_008_0078 mahatā krodhavegena trāsayann iva vīryavān
07_008_0079 tato haro jaṭī sthāṇur niśācarapatiḥ śivaḥ
07_008_0080 jagāma śaraṇaṁ devaṁ brahmāṇaṁ parameṣṭhinam
07_008_0081 tasminn āpatite sthāṇau prajānāṁ hitakāmyayā

07_008_0283 evaṁ bruvāṇaṁ devarṣiṁ nr̥patiḥ sr̥ñjayo ’bravīt
07_008_0284 mameyaṁ bhagavan kanyā matto varam abhīpsati
07_008_0285 nāradas tv abravīd enaṁ dehi mahyam imāṁ nr̥pa
07_008_0286 bhāryārthaṁ sumahac chreyaḥ prāptuṁ ced icchase ’nagha
07_008_0287 dadānīty eva saṁhr̥ṣṭaḥ sr̥ñjayaḥ prāha nāradam
07_008_0288 parvatas tu susaṁkruddho nāradaṁ vākyam abravīt
07_008_0289 hr̥dayena mayā pūrvaṁ vr̥tāṁ vai vr̥tavān asi
07_008_0290 yasmād vr̥tā tvayā vipra mā gāḥ svargaṁ yathecchayā
07_008_0291 evam ukto nāradas taṁ pratyuvācottaraṁ vacaḥ
07_008_0292 manovāgbuddhisaṁbhāṣāḥ satyaṁ toyam athāgnayaḥ
07_008_0293 pāṇigrahaṇamantrāś ca prathitaṁ varalakṣaṇam
07_008_0294 na tv eṣā niścitā niṣṭhā niṣṭhā saptapadī smr̥tā
07_008_0295 anutpanne ca kāryārthe māṁ tvaṁ vyāhr̥tavān asi
07_008_0296 tasmāt tvam api na svargaṁ gamiṣyasi mayā vinā
07_008_0297 anyonyam evaṁ śaptvā vai tasthatus tatra tau tadā
07_008_0298 sr̥ñjayo hy api vai viprān pānācchādanabhojanaiḥ
07_008_0299 putrakāmaḥ paraṁ śaktyā yatnenopācarac chuciḥ
07_008_0300 tasya prasannā viprendrāḥ kadā cit putram īpsavaḥ
07_008_0301 tapaḥsvādhyāyaniratā vedavedāṅgapāragāḥ
07_008_0302 sahitā nāradaṁ prāhur dehy asmai putram īpsitam
07_008_0303 tathety uktvā dvijair uktaḥ sr̥ñjayaṁ nārado ’bravīt
07_008_0304 varaṁ vr̥ṇīṣva bhadraṁ te yādr̥śaṁ putram īpsitam
07_008_0305 tathoktaḥ prāñjalī rājā putraṁ vavre guṇānvitam
07_008_0306 yaśasvinaṁ kīrtimantaṁ tejasvinam ariṁdamam
07_008_0307 yasya mūtraṁ purīṣaṁ ca kledaḥ svedaś ca kāñcanam
07_008_0308 suvarṇaṣṭhīvir ity eva tasya nāmābhavat kr̥tam
07_008_0309 tasmin varapradānena pravardhaty amite dhane
07_008_0310 kārayām āsa nr̥patiḥ sauvarṇaṁ sarvam īpsitam
07_008_0311 gr̥haprākāradurgāṇi brāhmaṇāvasathāny api
07_008_0312 śayyāsanāni yānāni sthālī piṭharabhājanam
07_008_0313 tasya sma rājño yad veśma bāhyāś copaskarāś ca ye
07_008_0314 sarvaṁ tat kāñcanamayaṁ kālena parivardhitam
07_008_0315 atha dasyugaṇāḥ śrutvā dr̥ṣṭvā cainaṁ tathāvidham
07_008_0316 saṁbhūya tasya nr̥pateḥ samārabdhāś cikīrṣitum
07_008_0317 ke cit tatrābruvan rājñaḥ putraṁ gr̥hṇīma vai svayam
07_008_0318 so ’syākaraḥ kāñcanasya tasya yatnaṁ carāmahe
07_008_0319 tatas te dasyavo lubdhāḥ praviśya nr̥pater gr̥ham
07_008_0320 rājaputraṁ tato jahruḥ suvarṇaṣṭīvinaṁ balāt
07_008_0321 gr̥hyainam anupāyajñā nayitvātha vanaṁ tataḥ
07_008_0322 hatvā viśasya nāpaśyan sulubdhā vasu kiṁ cana
07_008_0323 tasya prāṇair vimuktasya naṣṭaṁ tad varadaṁ vasu
07_008_0324 dasyavaś ca tadānyonyaṁ jaghnur mūrkhā vicetasaḥ
07_008_0325 hatvā parasparaṁ naṣṭāḥ kumāraṁ cādbhutaṁ bhuvi
07_008_0326 asaṁbhāvyaṁ gatā ghoraṁ narakaṁ duṣṭakāriṇaḥ
07_008_0327 taṁ dr̥ṣṭvā nihataṁ putraṁ varadattaṁ mahātapāḥ
07_008_0328 vilalāpa suduḥkhārto bahudhā karuṇaṁ nr̥paḥ
07_008_0329 vilapantaṁ niśamyātha putraśokahataṁ nr̥pam
07_008_0330 pratyadr̥śyata devarṣir nārado nr̥pasaṁnidhau
07_008_0331 uvāca cainaṁ duḥkhārtaṁ vilapantam acetasam
07_008_0332 sr̥ñjayaṁ nārado ’bhyetya tan nibodha yudhiṣṭhira
07_008_0333 kāmānām avitr̥ptas tvaṁ sr̥ñjayeha mariṣyasi
07_008_0334 yasya caite vayaṁ gehe uṣitā brahmavādinaḥ
07_008_0335 āvikṣitaṁ maruttaṁ ca mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0336 saṁvarto yājayām āsa spardhayā yaṁ br̥haspateḥ
07_008_0337 yasmai rājarṣaye prādād varaṁ sa bhagavān prabhuḥ
07_008_0338 haimaṁ himavataḥ pādaṁ yiyakṣor vividhaiḥ savaiḥ
07_008_0339 yasya sendrā marudgaṇā br̥haspatipurogamāḥ
07_008_0340 devā viśvasr̥jaḥ sarve yajñān ūhur mahātmanaḥ
07_008_0341 yajñavāṭasya sauvarṇāḥ sarve cāsan paricchadāḥ
07_008_0342 yasya sarvaṁ tadā hy annaṁ mano ’bhiprāyagaṁ śuci
07_008_0343 kāmato bubhujur viprāḥ sarve cānnārthino janāḥ
07_008_0344 payo dadhi ghr̥taṁ kṣaudraṁ bhakṣyaṁ bhojyaṁ ca śobhanam
07_008_0345 yasya yajñeṣu sarveṣu vāsāṁsy ābharaṇāni ca
07_008_0346 īpsitāny upatiṣṭhanti prasr̥tān vedapāragān
07_008_0347 marutaḥ pariveṣṭāro marut tasyābhavan gr̥he
07_008_0348 āvikṣitasya rājarṣer viśve devāḥ sabhāsadaḥ
07_008_0349 yasya vīryavato rājñaḥ suvr̥ṣṭyā sasyasaṁpadaḥ
07_008_0350 havirbhis tarpitā yena samyak kl̥ptair divaukasaḥ
07_008_0351 r̥ṣīṇāṁ ca pitr̥̄ṇāṁ ca devānāṁ sukhajīvinām
07_008_0352 brahmacaryaśrutasutair yajñair dānena cānr̥ṇaḥ
07_008_0353 śayanāsanayānāni svarṇarāśīś ca dustyajāḥ
07_008_0354 tat sarvam amitaṁ vittaṁ dattvā viprebhya icchayā
07_008_0355 so ’nudhyātas tu śakreṇa prajāḥ kr̥tvā nirāmayāḥ
07_008_0356 śraddadhāno jitām̐l lokān gataḥ puṇyaduho ’kṣayān
07_008_0357 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā
07_008_0358 putrāt puṇyataras tubhyaṁ mā putram anutapyathāḥ
07_008_0359 ayajvānam adākṣiṇyam adhi śvaityety udāharat

07_008_0360 suhotraṁ nāma rājānaṁ mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0361 ekavīram anādhr̥ṣyam amitragaṇamardanam
07_008_0362 yaḥ prāpya rājyaṁ dharmeṇa r̥tvigbrahmapurohitān
07_008_0363 apr̥cchad ātmanaḥ śreyaḥ pr̥ṣṭvā teṣāṁ mate sthitaḥ
07_008_0364 prajānāṁ pālanaṁ dharmo dānam ijyā dviṣajjayaḥ
07_008_0365 etat suhotro vijñāya dharmeṇaicchad dhanāgamam
07_008_0366 dharmeṇārādhayad devān bāṇaiḥ śatrūñ jayaṁs tathā
07_008_0367 sarvāṇy api ca bhūtāni svaguṇair apy arañjayat
07_008_0368 yo ’bhuṅktemāṁ vasumatīṁ mlecchāṭavikavarjitām
07_008_0369 yasmai vavarṣa parjanyo hiraṇyaṁ parivatsaram
07_008_0370 hiraṇyodās tathā nadyaḥ suhotrasya mahātmanaḥ
07_008_0371 yasmin kūrmān karkaṭakān matsyāṁś ca vividhān bahūn
07_008_0372 kāmān varṣati parjanyo rūpāṇi vividhāni ca
07_008_0373 sauvarṇāny aprameyāni vāpyaś ca krośasaṁmitāḥ
07_008_0374 sa tatra śatasāhasrān nakrān makarakacchapān
07_008_0375 sauvarṇān patitān dr̥ṣṭvā tato ’smayata vai ’tithiḥ
07_008_0376 tat suvarṇam aparyantaṁ rājarṣiḥ kurujāṅgale
07_008_0377 ījāno vitate yajñe brāhmaṇebhyo hy amanyata
07_008_0378 so ’śvamedhasahasreṇa rājasūyaśatena ca
07_008_0379 puṇyaiḥ kṣatriyayajñaiś ca prabhūtavaradakṣiṇaiḥ
07_008_0380 kāmyanaimittikājasrair iṣṭāṁ gatim avāptavān

07_008_0384 rājānaṁ pauravaṁ vīraṁ mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0385 yaḥ sahasraṁ sahasrāṇāṁ śvetān aśvān avāsr̥jat
07_008_0386 tasyāśvamedhe rājarṣer deśād deśāt sameyuṣām
07_008_0387 śikṣākṣaravidhijñānāṁ nāsīt saṁkhyā vipaścitām
07_008_0388 vedavidyāvratasnātā vadānyāḥ priyadarśanāḥ
07_008_0389 subhakṣācchādanagr̥hāḥ suśayyāsanavāhanāḥ
07_008_0390 naṭanartakagandharvaiḥ pūrṇakair vardhamānakaiḥ
07_008_0391 niyodhakaiś ca krīḍadbhis tatra sma pariharṣitāḥ
07_008_0392 yajñe yajñe yathākālaṁ dakṣiṇāḥ so ’tyakālayat
07_008_0393 dvipā daśasahasrākhyāḥ pramadāḥ kāñcanaprabhāḥ
07_008_0394 sadhvajāḥ sapatākāś ca rathā hemamayās tathā
07_008_0395 yaḥ sahasraṁ sahasrāṇi kanyāhemavibhūṣitāḥ
07_008_0396 dhūryujāśvagajārūḍhāḥ sagr̥hakṣetragośatāḥ
07_008_0397 śataṁ śatasahasrāṇi svarṇamālān atharṣabhān
07_008_0398 gavāṁ sahasrānucarān dakṣiṇā atyakālayat
07_008_0399 hemaśr̥ṅgyo raupyakhurāḥ savatsāḥ kāṁsyadohanāḥ
07_008_0400 dāsīdāsakharoṣṭrāṁś ca prādād ājāvikaṁ bahu
07_008_0401 ratnānāṁ vividhān kūṭān vividhān annaparvatān

07_008_0409 śibim auśīnaraṁ cāpi mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0410 ya imāṁ pr̥thivīṁ sarvāṁ carmavat paryaveṣṭayat
07_008_0411 sādridvīpārṇavavanāṁ rathaghoṣeṇa nādayan
07_008_0412 sa śibir vai ripūn nityaṁ mukhyān nighnan sapatnajit
07_008_0413 tena yajñair bahuvidhair iṣṭaṁ paryāptadakṣiṇaiḥ
07_008_0414 sa rājā vīryavān dhīmān avāpya vasu puṣkalam
07_008_0415 sarvamūrdhāvasiktānāṁ saṁmataḥ so ’bhavad yudhi
07_008_0416 ayajac cāśvamedhair yo vijitya pr̥thivīm imām
07_008_0417 nirargalair bahuphalair niṣkakoṭisahasradaḥ
07_008_0418 hastyaśvapaśubhir dhānyair mr̥gair gojāvibhis tathā
07_008_0419 vividhaiḥ pr̥thivīṁ pūrṇāṁ śibir brāhmaṇasāt karot
07_008_0420 yāvatyo varṣato dhārā yāvatyo divi tārakāḥ
07_008_0421 tāvatīr adadad gā vai śibir auśīnaro ’dhvare
07_008_0422 no yantāraṁ dhuras tasya kaṁ cid anyaṁ prajāpatiḥ
07_008_0423 bhūtaṁ bhavyaṁ bhaviṣyaṁ vā nādhyagacchan narottamam
07_008_0424 tasyāsan vividhā yajñāḥ sarvakāmaiḥ samanvitāḥ
07_008_0425 hemayūpāsanagr̥hā hemaprākāratoraṇāḥ
07_008_0426 śucisvādvannapānāś ca brāhmaṇāḥ prayutāyutāḥ
07_008_0427 nānābhakṣoccayataṭāḥ payodadhimadhuhradāḥ
07_008_0428 tasyāsan yajñavāṭeṣu nadyaḥ śubhrānnaparvatāḥ
07_008_0429 pibata snāta khādadhvam iti yatrocyate janaiḥ
07_008_0430 yasmai prādād varaṁ rudras tuṣṭaḥ puṇyena karmaṇā
07_008_0431 akṣayaṁ dadato vittaṁ śraddhā kīrtis tathā kriyāḥ

07_008_0437 rāmaṁ dāśarathiṁ caiva mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0438 yaṁ prajā anvamodanta pitā putrān ivaurasān
07_008_0439 asaṁkhyeyā guṇā yasmin nāsann amitatejasi
07_008_0440 yaś caturdaśavarṣāṇi nideśāt pitur acyutaḥ
07_008_0441 vane vanitayā sārdham avasal lakṣmaṇānugaḥ
07_008_0442 jaghāna ca janasthāne rākṣasān manujarṣabhaḥ
07_008_0443 tapasvināṁ rakṣaṇārthaṁ sahasrāṇi caturdaśa
07_008_0444 tatraiva vasatas tasya rāvaṇo nāma rākṣasaḥ
07_008_0445 jahāra bhāryāṁ vaidehīṁ saṁmohyainaṁ sahānujam
07_008_0446 jaghāna samare kruddhaḥ pureva tryambako ’ndhakam
07_008_0447 surāsurair avadhyaṁ taṁ devabrāhmaṇakaṇṭakam
07_008_0448 jaghāna sa mahābāhuḥ paulastyaṁ sagaṇaṁ raṇe
07_008_0449 saṁprāpya vidhivad rājyaṁ sarvabhūtānukampakaḥ
07_008_0450 ājahāra mahāyajñaṁ prajā dharmeṇa pālayan
07_008_0451 nirargalaṁ sajārūthyam aśvamedhaśataṁ vibhuḥ
07_008_0452 ājahāra sureśasya haviṣā mudam āvahan
07_008_0453 anyaiś ca vividhair yajñair īje bahuguṇair nr̥paḥ
07_008_0454 kṣutpipāse ’jayad rāmaḥ sarvarogāṁś ca dehinām
07_008_0455 satataṁ guṇasaṁpanno dīpyamānaḥ svatejasā
07_008_0456 ati sarvāṇi bhūtāni rāmo dāśarathir babhau
07_008_0457 r̥ṣīṇāṁ devatānāṁ ca mānuṣāṇāṁ ca sarvaśaḥ
07_008_0458 pr̥thivyāṁ sahavāso ’bhūd rāme rājyaṁ praśāsati
07_008_0459 nāhīyata tadā prāṇaḥ prāṇināṁ na tad anyathā
07_008_0460 prāṇāpānasamānāś ca rāme rājyaṁ praśāsati
07_008_0461 dīrghāyuṣaḥ prajāḥ sarvā yuvā na mriyate tadā
07_008_0462 vedaiś caturbhiḥ saṁprītāḥ prāpnuvanti divaukasaḥ
07_008_0463 havyaṁ kavyaṁ ca vividhaṁ niṣpūrtaṁ hutam eva ca
07_008_0464 adaṁśamaśakā deśā naṣṭavyālasarīsr̥pāḥ
07_008_0465 nāpsu prāṇabhr̥tāṁ mr̥tyur nākāle jvalano ’dahat
07_008_0466 adharmarucayo lubdhā mūrkhā vā nābhavaṁs tadā
07_008_0467 śiṣṭeṣṭaprājñakarmāṇaḥ sarvavarṇās tadābhavan
07_008_0468 svadhām ūrjaṁ ca rakṣobhir janasthāne praṇāśite
07_008_0469 prādān nihatya rakṣāṁsi pitr̥devebhya īśvaraḥ
07_008_0470 sahasraputrāḥ puruṣā daśavarṣaśatāyuṣaḥ
07_008_0471 na ca jyeṣṭāḥ kaniṣṭebhyas tadā śrāddhān akārayan
07_008_0472 śyāmo yuvā lohitākṣo mattamātaṅgavikramaḥ
07_008_0473 ājānubāhuḥ subhujaḥ siṁhaskandho mahābalaḥ
07_008_0474 daśavarṣasahasrāṇi daśavarṣaśatāni ca
07_008_0475 sarvabhūtamanaḥkānto rāmo rājyam akārayat
07_008_0476 rāmo rāmo rāma iti prajānām abhavat kathā

07_008_0483 bhagīrathaṁ ca rājānaṁ mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0484 yena bhāgīrathī gaṅgā cayanaiḥ kāñcanaiś citā
07_008_0485 yaḥ sahasraṁ sahasrāṇāṁ kanyā hemavibhūṣitāḥ
07_008_0486 rājñaś ca rājaputrāṁś ca brāhmaṇebhyo hy amanyata
07_008_0487 sarvā rathagatāḥ kanyā rathāḥ sarve caturyujaḥ
07_008_0488 rathe rathe śataṁ nāgāḥ padmino hemamālinaḥ
07_008_0489 sahasram aśvāś caikaikaṁ gajānāṁ pr̥ṣṭhato ’nvayuḥ
07_008_0490 aśve aśve śataṁ gāvo gavāṁ paścād ajāvikam
07_008_0491 tenākrāntā janaughena dakṣiṇā bhūyasīr dadat
07_008_0492 upahvare ’tivyathitā tasyāṅke niṣasāda ha
07_008_0493 tathā bhāgīrathī gaṅgā urvaśī cābhavat purā
07_008_0494 duhitr̥tvaṁ gatā rājñaḥ putratvam agamat tadā
07_008_0495 tatra gāthāṁ jaguḥ prītā gandharvāḥ sūryavarcasaḥ
07_008_0496 pitr̥devamanuṣyāṇāṁ śr̥ṇvatāṁ valguvādinām
07_008_0497 bhagīrathaṁ yajamānam aikṣvākuṁ bhūridakṣiṇam
07_008_0498 gaṅgā samudragā devī vavre pitaram īśvaram
07_008_0499 tasya sendraiḥ savaruṇair devair yajñaḥ svalaṁkr̥taḥ
07_008_0500 samyak parigr̥hītaś ca śāntavighno nirāmayaḥ
07_008_0501 yo ya iccheta vipro vai yatra yatrātmanaḥ priyam
07_008_0502 bhagīrathas tadā prītas tatra tatrādadad vaśī
07_008_0503 nādeyaṁ brāhmaṇeṣv āsīd asya kiṁ cit priyaṁ dhanam
07_008_0504 so ’pi vipraprasādena brahmalokaṁ gato nr̥paḥ
07_008_0505 yena yātā makhamukhe diśāśāv iha pādapāḥ
07_008_0506 tenāvasthātum icchanti taṁ gatvā rājam īśvaram
07_008_0507 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā
07_008_0508 putrāt puṇyataras tubhyaṁ mā putram anutapyathāḥ
07_008_0509 ayajvānam adākṣiṇyam adhi śvaityety udāharat
07_008=0509 Colophon.
07_008=0509 nārada uvāca
07_008_0510 dilīpaṁ ced ailavilaṁ mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0511 yasya yajñaśateṣv āsan prayutāyutaśo dvijāḥ
07_008_0512 tattvajñānārthasaṁpannā yajvānaḥ putrapautriṇaḥ
07_008_0513 ya imāṁ vasusaṁpūrṇāṁ vasudhāṁ vasudhādhipaḥ
07_008_0514 ījāno vitate yajñe brāhmaṇebhyo hy amanyata
07_008_0515 dilīpasya ca yajñeṣu kr̥taḥ panthā hiraṇmayaḥ
07_008_0516 taṁ dharma iva kurvāṇāḥ sendradevāḥ samāgaman
07_008_0517 caṣālaṁ pracaṣālaṁ ca yasya yūpe hiraṇmaye
07_008_0518 yad yoṣā hemasaṁchannā mattāḥ pathiṣu śerate
07_008_0519 tad etad adbhutaṁ manye anyair na sadr̥śaṁ nr̥paiḥ
07_008_0520 yad apsu yudhyamānasya cakre na paripetatuḥ
07_008_0521 rājānaṁ dr̥ḍhadhanvānaṁ dilīpaṁ satyavādinam
07_008_0522 ye ’paśyan bhūridākṣiṇyaṁ te ’pi svargajito narāḥ
07_008_0523 trayaḥ śabdā na jīryante khaṭvāṅgasya niveśane
07_008_0524 svādhyāyaghoṣo jyāghoṣaḥ pibatāśnīta khādata
07_008_0525 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā
07_008_0526 putrāt puṇyataras tubhyaṁ mā putram anutapyathāḥ
07_008_0527 ayajvānam adākṣiṇyam adhi śvaityety udāharat
07_008=0527 Colophon.
07_008=0527 nārada uvāca
07_008_0528 māndhātā ced yauvanāśvas trailokyavijayī mr̥taḥ
07_008_0529 yaṁ devāv aśvinau garbhāt pituḥ pūrvaṁ cakarṣatuḥ
07_008_0530 mr̥gayāṁ vicaran rājā tr̥ṣitaḥ klāntavāhanaḥ
07_008_0531 dhūmaṁ dr̥ṣṭvāgamat satraṁ pr̥ṣadājyam avāpa saḥ
07_008_0532 taṁ dr̥ṣṭvā yuvanāśvasya jaṭhare sūnutāṁ gatam
07_008_0533 garbhād dhi jahratur devāv aśvinau bhiṣajāṁ varau
07_008_0534 taṁ dr̥ṣṭvā pitur utsaṅge śayānaṁ devavarcasam
07_008_0535 mām evāyaṁ dhayatv agre iti ha smāha vāsavaḥ
07_008_0536 tato ’ṅgulibhyo hīndrasya prādur āsīt payo ’mr̥tam
07_008_0537 māṁ dhāsyatīti kāruṇyād yad indro hy anvakampayat
07_008_0538 tasmāt sa māndhātety evaṁ nāma tasyādbhutaṁ kr̥tam
07_008_0539 tatas tu dhārāḥ payaso ghr̥tasya ca mahātmanaḥ
07_008_0540 tasyāsye yauvanāśvasya pāṇir indrasya cāsravat
07_008_0541 apibat pāṇim indrasya sa cāpy ahnābhyavardhata
07_008_0542 so ’bhavad dvādaśasamo dvādaśāhena vīryavān
07_008_0543 imāṁ ca pr̥thivīṁ kr̥tsnām ekāhnā sa vyajījayat
07_008_0544 dharmātmā dhr̥timān vīraḥ satyasaṁdho jitendriyaḥ
07_008_0545 janamejayaṁ sudhanvānaṁ gayaṁ pūruṁ br̥hadratham
07_008_0546 asitaṁ caiva rāmaṁ ca māndhātā manujo ’jayat
07_008_0547 udeti ca yataḥ sūryo yatra ca pratitiṣṭhati
07_008_0548 tat sarvaṁ yauvanāśvasya māndhātuḥ kṣetram ucyate
07_008_0549 so ’śvamedhaśatair iṣṭvā rājasūyaśatena ca
07_008_0550 adadad rohitān aśvān brāhmaṇebhyo viśāṁ pate
07_008_0551 hairaṇyān yojanotsedhān āyatāñ śatayojanam
07_008_0552 bahuprakārān susvādūn bhakṣyabhojyānnaparvatān
07_008_0553 atiriktaṁ brāhmaṇebhyo bhuñjāno hīyate janaḥ
07_008_0554 bhakṣyānnapānanicayāḥ śuśubhus tv annaparvatāḥ
07_008_0555 ghr̥tahradāḥ sūpapaṅkā dadhiphenā guḍodakāḥ
07_008_0556 rurudhuḥ parvatān nadyo madhukṣīravahāḥ śubhāḥ
07_008_0557 devāsurā narā yakṣā gandharvoragapakṣiṇaḥ
07_008_0558 viprās tatrāgatāś cāsan vedavedāṅgapāragāḥ
07_008_0559 brāhmaṇā r̥ṣayaś cāpi nāsaṁs tatrāvipaścitaḥ
07_008_0560 samudrāntāṁ vasumatīṁ vasupūrṇāṁ tu sarvataḥ
07_008_0561 sa tāṁ brāhmaṇasāt kr̥tvā jagāmāstaṁ tadā nr̥paḥ
07_008_0562 gataḥ puṇyakr̥tāṁ lokān vyāpya svayaśasā diśaḥ
07_008_0563 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā
07_008_0564 putrāt puṇyataras tubhyaṁ mā putram anutapyathāḥ
07_008_0565 ayajvānam adākṣiṇyam adhi śvaityety udāharat
07_008=0565 Colophon.
07_008=0565 nārada uvāca
07_008_0566 yayātiṁ nāhuṣaṁ caiva mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0567 rājasūyaśatair iṣṭvā so ’śvamedhaśatena ca
07_008_0568 puṇḍarīkasahasreṇa vājapeyaśatais tathā
07_008_0569 atirātrasahasreṇa cāturmāsyaiś ca kāmataḥ
07_008_0570 agniṣṭomaiś ca vividhaiḥ satraiś ca prājyadakṣiṇaiḥ
07_008_0571 abrāhmaṇānāṁ yad vittaṁ pr̥thivyām asti kiṁ cana
07_008_0572 tat sarvaṁ parisaṁkhyāya tato brāhmaṇasāt karot
07_008_0573 vyūḍhe devāsure yuddhe kr̥tvā devasahāyatām
07_008_0574 caturdhā vyabhajat sarvāṁ caturbhyaḥ pr̥thivīm imām
07_008_0575 yajñair nānāvidhair iṣṭvā prajām utpādya cottamām
07_008_0576 devayānyāṁ cauśanasyāṁ śarmiṣṭhāyāṁ ca dharmataḥ
07_008_0577 devāraṇyeṣu sarveṣu vijahārāmaropamaḥ
07_008_0578 ātmanaḥ kāmacāreṇa dvitīya iva vāsavaḥ
07_008_0579 yadā nādhyagamat so ’ntaṁ kāmānāṁ sarvavedavit
07_008_0580 tato gāthām imāṁ gītvā sadāraḥ prāviśad vanam
07_008_0581 yat pr̥thivyāṁ vrīhiyavaṁ hiraṇyaṁ paśavaḥ striyaḥ
07_008_0582 nālam ekasya tat sarvam iti matvā śamaṁ vrajet
07_008_0583 evaṁ kāmān parityajya yayātir dhr̥tim etya ca
07_008_0584 pūruṁ rājye pratiṣṭhāpya prayāto vanam īśvaraḥ
07_008_0585 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā
07_008_0586 putrāt puṇyataras tubhyaṁ mā putram anutapyathāḥ
07_008_0587 ayajvānam adākṣiṇyam adhi śvaityety udāharat
07_008=0587 Colophon.
07_008=0587 nārada uvāca
07_008_0588 nābhāgam ambarīṣaṁ ca mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0589 yaḥ sahasraṁ sahasrāṇāṁ rājñāṁ caikas tv ayodhayat
07_008_0590 jigīṣamāṇāḥ saṁgrāme samantād vairiṇo ’bhyayuḥ
07_008_0591 śastrayuddhavido ghorāḥ sr̥jantaś cāśivā giraḥ
07_008_0592 balalābhād vaśīkr̥tya teṣāṁ śastrabalena ca
07_008_0593 chattrāyudhadhvajarathāṁś chittvā prāsān gatavyathaḥ
07_008_0594 ta enaṁ muktasaṁnāhā nāthanto jīvitaiṣiṇaḥ
07_008_0595 śaraṇyam īyuḥ śaraṇaṁ tavāsma iti vādinaḥ
07_008_0596 sa tu tān vaśagān kr̥tvā jitvā cemāṁ vasuṁdharām
07_008_0597 īje yajñaśatair iṣṭair yathā śakras tathānagha
07_008_0598 bubhujuḥ sarvasaṁpannam annam anye janās tadā
07_008_0599 tasya yajñeṣu viprendrāḥ sutr̥ptāḥ paramārcitāḥ
07_008_0600 modakān pūrikāpūpān dhānā vaṭakaśaṣkulīḥ
07_008_0601 karambhān pr̥thukān manthān anyāni sukr̥tāni ca
07_008_0602 sūpān maireyakān yūṣān rāgaṣāṇḍavapānakān
07_008_0603 mr̥ṣṭānnāni suyuktāni mr̥dūni surabhīṇi ca
07_008_0604 ghr̥taṁ madhu payas toyaṁ dadhīni rasavanti ca
07_008_0605 phalaṁ mūlaṁ ca susvādu dvijās tatropabhujyate
07_008_0606 madanīyāni pānāni viditvā cātmanaḥ sukham
07_008_0607 apibanta yathākāmaṁ pānapā gītavāditaiḥ
07_008_0608 tatra sma gāthā gāyanti kṣībā hr̥ṣṭāḥ paṭhanti ca
07_008_0609 nābhāgastutisaṁyuktā nanr̥tuś ca sahasraśaḥ
07_008_0610 teṣu yajñeṣv ambarīṣo dakṣiṇām atyakālayat
07_008_0611 rājñāṁ daśasahasrāṇi daśa prayutayājinām
07_008_0612 hiraṇyakavacān sarvāñ śvetacchattraprakīrṇakān
07_008_0613 hiraṇyasyandanārūḍhān sānuyātraparicchadān
07_008_0614 ījāno vitate yajñe dakṣiṇām atyakālayat
07_008_0615 mūrdhābhiṣiktāṁś ca nr̥pān rājaputraśatāni ca
07_008_0616 sadaṇḍaṁ kośanicayān brāhmaṇebhyo hy amanyata
07_008_0617 naiva pūrve janāś cakrur na kariṣyanti cāpare
07_008_0618 yad ambarīṣo nr̥patiḥ karoty amitadakṣiṇaḥ
07_008_0619 ity evam anvamodanta prītā yasya maharṣayaḥ
07_008_0620 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā
07_008_0621 putrāt puṇyataras tubhyaṁ mā putram anutapyathāḥ
07_008_0622 ayajvānam adākṣiṇyam adhi śvaityety udāharat

07_008_0623 śaśabinduṁ ca rājānaṁ mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0624 īje sa vividhair yajñaiḥ śrīmān satyaparākramaḥ
07_008_0625 tasya bhāryāsahasrāṇāṁ śatam āsīn mahātmanaḥ
07_008_0626 ekaikasyāṁ ca bhāryāyāṁ sahasraṁ tanayābhavan
07_008_0627 te kumārāḥ parākrāntāḥ sarve niyutayājinaḥ
07_008_0628 rājānaḥ kratubhir mukhyair ījānā vedapāragāḥ
07_008_0629 hiraṇyakavacāḥ sarve sarve cottamadhanvinaḥ
07_008_0630 sarve ’śvamedhair ījānāḥ kumārāḥ śāśabindavaḥ
07_008_0631 tān aśvamedhe rājendro brāhmaṇebhyo ’dadat pitā
07_008_0632 śataṁ śataṁ rathagatā ekaikaṁ pr̥ṣṭhato ’nvayuḥ
07_008_0633 rājaputraṁ tadā kanyās tapanīyasvalaṁkr̥tāḥ
07_008_0634 kanyāṁ kanyāṁ śataṁ nāgā nāge nāge śataṁ rathāḥ
07_008_0635 rathe rathe śataṁ cāśvā balino hemamālinaḥ
07_008_0636 aśve aśve sahasraṁ gā gavāṁ paścād ajāvikam
07_008_0637 etad dhanam aparyantam aśvamedhe mahāmakhe
07_008_0638 śaśabindur mahābhāgo brāhmaṇebhyo hy amanyata
07_008_0639 vārkṣāś ca yūpā yāvanto aśvamedhe mahāmakhe
07_008_0640 te tathaiva punaś cānye tāvantaḥ kāñcanābhavan
07_008_0641 bhakṣyānnapānanicayāḥ parvatāḥ krośam ucchritāḥ
07_008_0642 tasyāśvamedhe nirvr̥tte rājñaḥ śiṣṭās trayodaśa
07_008_0643 tuṣṭapuṣṭajanākīrṇāṁ śāntavighnām anāmayām
07_008_0644 śaśabindur imāṁ bhūmiṁ ciraṁ bhuktvā divaṁ gataḥ
07_008_0645 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā

07_008_0648 gayaṁ cāmūrtarayasaṁ mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0649 yo vai varṣaśataṁ rājā hutaśiṣṭāśano ’bhavat
07_008_0650 tasmai hy agnir varaṁ prādāt tato vavre varaṁ gayaḥ
07_008_0651 tapasā brahmacaryeṇa vratena niyamena ca
07_008_0652 gurūṇāṁ ca prasādena vedān icchāmi veditum
07_008_0653 svadharmeṇāvihiṁsyānyān dhanam icchāmi cākṣayam
07_008_0654 vipreṣu dadataś caiva śraddhā bhavatu nityaśaḥ
07_008_0655 ananyāsu savarṇāsu putrajanma ca me bhavet
07_008_0656 annaṁ me dadataḥ śraddhā dharme me ramatāṁ manaḥ
07_008_0657 avighnaṁ cāstu me nityaṁ dharmakāryeṣu pāvaka
07_008_0658 tathā bhaviṣyatīty uktvā tatraivāntaradhīyata
07_008_0659 gayo ’py avāpya tat sarvaṁ dharmeṇārīn ajījayat
07_008_0660 sa darśapaurṇamāsābhyāṁ kāleṣv āgrayaṇena ca
07_008_0661 cāturmāsyaiś ca vividhair yajñaiś cāvāptadakṣiṇaiḥ
07_008_0662 ayajac chraddhayā rājā parisaṁvatsaraṁ śatam
07_008_0663 daśa nāgasahasrāṇi śatam aśvaśatāni ca
07_008_0664 śataṁ niṣkasahasrāṇi gavāṁ cāpy ayutāni ṣaṭ
07_008_0665 utthāyotthāya sa prādāt parisaṁvatsaraṁ śatam
07_008_0666 nakṣatreṣu ca sarveṣu dadan nakṣatradakṣiṇāḥ
07_008_0667 īje ca vividhair yajñair yathā somo ’ṅgirā yathā
07_008_0668 sauvarṇāṁ pr̥thivīṁ kr̥tvā ya imāṁ maṇiśarkarām
07_008_0669 viprebhyaḥ prādadad rājā so ’śvamedhe mahāmakhe
07_008_0670 jāmbūnadamayā yūpāḥ sarve ratnaparicchadāḥ
07_008_0671 gayasyāsan samr̥ddhās tu sarvabhūtamanoharāḥ
07_008_0672 sarvakāmasamr̥ddhāṁś ca prādāt tāṁś ca gayas tadā
07_008_0673 brāhmaṇebhyaḥ prahr̥ṣṭebhyaḥ sarvabhūtebhya eva ca
07_008_0674 samudradvīpaśaileṣu nadīnadavaneṣu ca
07_008_0675 nagareṣu ca rāṣṭreṣu divi vyomni ca ye ’vasan
07_008_0676 bhūtagrāmāś ca vividhāḥ saṁtr̥ptā yajñasaṁpadā
07_008_0677 gayasya sadr̥śo yajño nāsty anya iti te ’bruvan
07_008_0678 ṣaṭtriṁśadyojanāyāmā triṁśadyojanam āyatā
07_008_0679 paścāt puraś caturviṁśad vedī hy āsīd dhiraṇmayī
07_008_0680 gayasya yajamānasya muktāvajramaṇistr̥tā
07_008_0681 prādāt sa brāhmaṇebhyo ’tha vāsāṁsyābharaṇāni ca
07_008_0682 yathoktā dakṣiṇāś cānyā viprebhyo bhūridakṣiṇaḥ
07_008_0683 yatra bhojanaśiṣṭasya parvatāḥ pañcaviṁśatiḥ
07_008_0684 kulyāḥ kr̥śaravāhinyo rasānām abhavaṁs tadā
07_008_0685 vastrābharaṇagandhānāṁ rāśayaś ca pr̥thagvidhāḥ
07_008_0686 yasya prabhāvāc ca gayas triṣu lokeṣu viśrutaḥ
07_008_0687 vaṭaś cākṣayyakaraṇaḥ puṇyaṁ brahmasaraś ca tat
07_008_0688 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā

07_008_0691 sāṁkr̥tiṁ rantidevaṁ ca mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0692 yasya dviśatasāhasrā āsan sūdā mahātmanaḥ
07_008_0693 gr̥hān abhyāgatān viprān atithīn pariveṣakāḥ
07_008_0694 pakvāpakvaṁ divārātraṁ varānnam amr̥topamam
07_008_0695 nyāyenādhigataṁ vittaṁ brāhmaṇebhyo hy amanyata
07_008_0696 vedān adhītya dharmeṇa yaś cakre dviṣato vaśe
07_008_0697 upasthitāś ca paśavaḥ svayaṁ yaṁ saṁśitavratam
07_008_0698 bahavaḥ svargam icchanto vidhivat satrayājinam
07_008_0699 nadī mahānasād yasya pravr̥ttā carmarāśitaḥ
07_008_0700 tasmāc carmaṇvatī pūrvam agnihotre ’bhavat purā
07_008_0701 brāhmaṇebhyo dadau niṣkān sauvarṇān sa prabhāvataḥ
07_008_0702 tubhyaṁ niṣkaṁ tubhyaṁ niṣkam iti ha sma prabhāṣate
07_008_0703 tubhyaṁ tubhyam iti prādān niṣkān niṣkān sahasraśaḥ
07_008_0704 tataḥ punaḥ samāśvāsya niṣkān eva prayacchati
07_008_0705 alpaṁ dattaṁ mayādyeti niṣkakoṭiṁ pradāya saḥ
07_008_0706 ekāhnā dāsyati punaḥ ko ’nyas tat saṁpradāsyati
07_008_0707 sahasraśaś ca sauvarṇān vr̥ṣabhān gośatānugān
07_008_0708 adhyardhamāsam adadad brāhmaṇebhyaḥ śataṁ samāḥ
07_008_0709 agnihotropakaraṇaṁ yajñopakaraṇaṁ ca yat
07_008_0710 r̥ṣibhyaḥ karakān kumbhān sthālīpiṭharam eva ca
07_008_0711 śayanāsanayānāni prāsādāṁś ca gr̥hāṇi ca
07_008_0712 vr̥kṣāṁś ca vividhān dadyād annāni ca dhanāni ca
07_008_0713 sarvaṁ sauvarṇam evāsīd rantidevasya dhīmataḥ
07_008_0714 tatrāsya gāthā gāyanti ye purāṇavido janāḥ
07_008_0715 rantidevasya tāṁ dr̥ṣṭvā samr̥ddhim atimānuṣīm
07_008_0716 naitādr̥śaṁ dr̥ṣṭapūrvaṁ kuberasadaneṣv api
07_008_0717 dhanaṁ ca pūryamāṇaṁ naḥ kiṁ punar manujeṣv iti
07_008_0718 vyaktaṁ vasvokasāreyam ity ūcus tatra vismitāḥ
07_008_0719 sāṁkr̥te rantidevasya yāṁ rātrim atithiṁ vaset
07_008_0720 ālabhyanta tadā gāvaḥ sahasrāṇy ekaviṁśatiḥ
07_008_0721 tatra sma sūdāḥ krośanti sumr̥ṣṭamaṇikuṇḍalāḥ
07_008_0722 sūpabhūyiṣṭam aśnīdhvaṁ nādya māṁsaṁ yathāpuram
07_008_0723 rantidevasya yat kiṁ cit sauvarṇam abhavat tadā
07_008_0724 tat sarvaṁ vitate yajñe brāhmaṇebhyo hy amanyata
07_008_0725 pratyakṣaṁ tasya havyāni pratigr̥hṇanti devatāḥ
07_008_0726 kavyāni pitaraḥ kāle sarvakāmān dvijottamāḥ
07_008_0727 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā

07_008_0730 dauṣyantiṁ bharataṁ cāpi mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0731 karmāṇy asukarāṇy anyaiḥ kr̥tavān yaḥ śiśur vane
07_008_0732 hemāvadātān yaḥ siṁhān nakhadaṁṣṭrāyudhān balī
07_008_0733 nirvīryāṁs tarasā kr̥tvā vicakarṣa babandha ca
07_008_0734 krūrāṁś cograbalān vyāghrān damitvā cākarod vaśe
07_008_0735 manaḥśilā iva śilāḥ saṁyuktā jaturāśibhiḥ
07_008_0736 vyālādīṁś cātibalavāṁs tatpratīpān gajān api
07_008_0737 daṁṣṭrāsu gr̥hyāvaruhya śuṣkāsyān akarod vaśe
07_008_0738 mahiṣān apy atibalān balena vicakarṣa ha
07_008_0739 balinaḥ sr̥marān khaḍgān nānāsattvāni cāpy uta
07_008_0740 kr̥cchraprāṇān vane baddhvā damayitvāpy avāsr̥jat
07_008_0741 taṁ sarvadamanety āhus tadvidas tena karmaṇā
07_008_0742 taṁ pratyaṣedhaj jananī mā sattvāni vijījahi
07_008_0743 so ’śvamedhaśateneṣṭvā yamunām anu vīryavān
07_008_0744 triśatāśvān sarasvatyāṁ gaṅgām anu catuḥśatān
07_008_0745 so ’śvamedhasahasreṇa rājasūyaśatena ca
07_008_0746 punar īje mahāyajñaiḥ samāptavaradakṣiṇaiḥ
07_008_0747 agniṣṭomātirātrāṇām ukthyaviśvajitām api
07_008_0748 vājapeyeṣṭisatrāṇāṁ sahasraiś ca susaṁbhr̥taiḥ
07_008_0749 iṣṭvā śākuntalo rājā tarpayitvā dvijān dhanaiḥ
07_008_0750 sahasraṁ yatra padmānāṁ kaṇvāya bharato dadau
07_008_0751 jāmbūnadasya śuddhasya kanakasya mahāyaśāḥ
07_008_0752 yasya yūpāḥ śatavyāmāḥ pariṇāhena kāñcanāḥ
07_008_0753 samāgamya dvijaiḥ sārdhaṁ sendrair devaiḥ samucchritāḥ
07_008_0754 svalaṁkr̥tā bhrājamānāḥ sarvaratnair manoramaiḥ
07_008_0755 hiraṇyam aśvān dviradān rathān uṣṭrān ajāvikān
07_008_0756 dāsīdāsaṁ dhanaṁ dhānyaṁ gāḥ savatsāḥ payasvinīḥ
07_008_0757 grāmān gr̥hāṇi kṣetrāṇi vividhāṁś ca paricchadān
07_008_0758 koṭīśatāyutaṁ caiva brāhmaṇebhyo hy amanyata
07_008_0759 cakravartī hy adīnātmā jetāris tv ajitaḥ paraiḥ
07_008_0760 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā
07_008_0761 putrāt puṇyataras tubhyaṁ mā putram anutapyathāḥ
07_008_0762 ayajvānam adākṣiṇyam adhi śvaityety udāharat
07_008=0762 Colophon.
07_008=0762 nārada uvāca
07_008_0763 pr̥thuṁ vainyaṁ ca rājānaṁ mr̥taṁ sr̥ñjaya śuśruma
07_008_0764 yam abhyaṣiñcan sāmrājye rājasūye maharṣayaḥ
07_008_0765 ayaṁ naḥ prathayiṣyeta sarvān ity abhavat pr̥thuḥ
07_008_0766 kṣatān nas trāsyate sarvān ity evaṁ kṣatriyo ’bhavat
07_008_0767 pr̥thuṁ vainyaṁ prajā dr̥ṣṭvā raktāḥ smeti yad abruvan
07_008_0768 tato rājeti nāmāsya anurāgād ajāyata
07_008_0769 akr̥ṣṭapacyā pr̥thivī āsīd vainyasya kāmadhuk
07_008_0770 sarvāḥ kumbhaduho gāvaḥ puṭake puṭake madhu
07_008_0771 āsan hiraṇmayā darbhāḥ sukhasparśāḥ sukhāvahāḥ
07_008_0772 teṣāṁ cīrāṇi saṁvītāḥ prajās teṣv eva śerate
07_008_0773 phalāny amr̥takalpāni mūlāni ca madhūni ca
07_008_0774 teṣām āsīt tadāhāro nirāhārāś ca nābhavan
07_008_0775 arogāḥ sarvasiddhārthā manuṣyā akutobhayāḥ
07_008_0776 nyavasanta yathākāmaṁ vr̥kṣeṣu ca guhāsu ca
07_008_0777 pravibhāgo na rāṣṭrāṇāṁ purāṇāṁ cābhavat tadā
07_008_0778 yathāsukhaṁ yathākāmaṁ tathaitā muditāḥ prajāḥ
07_008_0779 tasya saṁstambhayann āpaḥ samudram abhiyāsyataḥ
07_008_0780 parvatāś ca dadur mārgaṁ dhvajabhaṅgaś ca nābhavat
07_008_0781 taṁ vanaspatayaḥ śailā devāsuranaroragāḥ
07_008_0782 saptarṣayaḥ puṇyajanā gandharvāpsaraso ’pi ca
07_008_0783 pitaraś ca sukhāsīnam abhigamyedam abruvan
07_008_0784 samrāḍ asi kṣatriyo ’si rājā goptā pitāsi naḥ
07_008_0785 dehy asmabhyaṁ mahārāja prabhuḥ sann īpsitān varān
07_008_0786 yair vayaṁ śāśvatīs tr̥ptīr vartayiṣyāmahe sukham
07_008_0787 tathety uktvā pr̥thur vainyo gr̥hītvājagavaṁ dhanuḥ
07_008_0788 śarāṁś cāpratimān ghorāṁś cintayitvābravīn mahīm
07_008_0789 ehy ehi vasudhe kṣipraṁ kṣaraibhyaḥ kāṅkṣitaṁ payaḥ
07_008_0790 tato dāsyāmi bhadraṁ te annaṁ yasya yathepsitam
07_008=0790 vasudhovāca
07_008_0791 duhitr̥tvena māṁ vīra saṁkalpayitum arhasi
07_008=0791 nārada uvāca
07_008_0792 tathety uktvā pr̥thuḥ sarvaṁ vidhānam akarod vaśī
07_008_0793 tato bhūtanikāyās te vasudhāṁ duduhus tadā
07_008_0794 tāṁ vanaspatayaḥ pūrvaṁ samuttasthur dudhukṣavaḥ
07_008_0795 sātiṣṭhad vatsalā vatsaṁ dogdhr̥̄n pātrāṇi cecchatī
07_008_0796 vatso ’bhūt puṣpitaḥ śālaḥ plakṣo dogdhābhavat tadā
07_008_0797 chinnaprarohaṇaṁ dugdhaṁ pātram audumbaraṁ śubham
07_008_0798 udayaḥ parvato vatso merur dogdhā mahāgiriḥ
07_008_0799 ratnāny oṣadhayo dugdhaṁ pātram aśmamayaṁ tathā
07_008_0800 dogdhā cāsīt tadā devo dugdham ūrjaskaraṁ priyam
07_008_0801 asurā duduhur māyām āmapātre tu te tadā
07_008_0802 dogdhā dvimūrdhā tatrāsīd vatsaś cāsīd virocanaḥ
07_008_0803 kr̥ṣiṁ ca sasyaṁ ca narā duduhuḥ pr̥thivītale
07_008_0804 svāyaṁbhuvo manur vatsas teṣāṁ dogdhābhavat pr̥thuḥ
07_008_0805 alābupātre ca tathā viṣaṁ dugdhā vasuṁdharā
07_008_0806 dhr̥tarāṣṭro ’bhavad dogdhā teṣāṁ vatsas tu takṣakaḥ
07_008_0807 saptarṣibhir brahma dugdhā tathā cākliṣṭakarmabhiḥ
07_008_0808 dogdhā br̥haspatiḥ pātraṁ chando vatsas tu somarāṭ
07_008_0809 antardhānaṁ cāmapātre dugdhā puṇyajanair virāṭ
07_008_0810 dogdhā vaiśravaṇas teṣāṁ vatsa āsīt kuberakaḥ
07_008_0811 puṇyagandhān padmapātre gandharvāpsaraso ’duhan
07_008_0812 vatsaś citrarathas teṣāṁ dogdhā viśvaruciḥ prabhuḥ
07_008_0813 svadhāṁ rajatapātre tu duduhuḥ pitaraś ca tām
07_008_0814 vatso ’tra vatsaras teṣāṁ yamo dogdhā tathāntakaḥ
07_008_0815 evaṁ nikāyais tair dugdhā payo ’bhīṣṭaṁ hi sā virāṭ
07_008_0816 yair vartayanti te hy adya pātrair vatsaiś ca nityaśaḥ
07_008_0817 sa yajñair vividhair iṣṭvā pr̥thur vainyaḥ pratāpavān
07_008_0818 saṁtarpayitvā bhūtāni sarvaiḥ kāmair manaḥpriyaiḥ
07_008_0819 hairaṇyān akarod rājā ye ke cit pārthivā bhuvi
07_008_0820 tān brāhmaṇebhyaḥ prāyacchad aśvamedhe mahāmakhe
07_008_0821 ṣaṣṭināgasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭināgaśatāni ca
07_008_0822 sauvarṇān akarod rājā brāhmaṇebhyaś ca tāṁ dadau
07_008_0823 imāṁ ca pr̥thivīṁ sarvāṁ maṇiratnavibhūṣitām
07_008_0824 sauvarṇīm akarod rājā brāhmaṇebhyaś ca tān dadau
07_008_0825 sa cen mamāra sr̥ñjaya caturbhadrataras tvayā
07_008_0826 putrāt puṇyataras tubhyaṁ mā putram anutapyathāḥ
07_008_0827 ayajvānam adākṣiṇyam adhi śvaityety udāharat
07_008=0827 Colophon.
07_008=0827 nārada uvāca
07_008_0828 rāmo mahātapāḥ śūro vīralokanamaskr̥taḥ
07_008_0829 jāmadagnyo ’py atiyaśā avitr̥pto mariṣyati
07_008_0830 yasyābhram anuparyeti bhūmiṁ kurvan vipāṁsulām
07_008_0831 na cāsīd vikriyā yasya prāpya śriyam anuttamām
07_008_0832 yaḥ kṣatriyaiḥ parāmr̥ṣṭe vatse pitari cukrudhe
07_008_0833 tato ’vadhīt kārtavīryam ajitaṁ samare paraiḥ
07_008_0834 kṣatriyāṇāṁ catuḥṣaṣṭim ayutāni sahasraśaḥ
07_008_0835 tadā mr̥tyoḥ sametāni ekaikaṁ dhanuṣājayat
07_008_0836 brahmadviṣāṁ cātha tasmin sahasrāṇi caturdaśa
07_008_0837 punar anyāni jagrāha dantakrūre jaghāna ha
07_008_0838 sahasraṁ musalenāghnan sahasram asināvadhīt
07_008_0839 udbandhanāt sahasraṁ ca haihayāḥ samare hatāḥ
07_008_0840 sarathāśvagajā vīrā nihatās tatra śerate
07_008_0841 pitur vadhāmarṣitena jāmadagnyena dhīmatā
07_008_0842 nijaghne daśasāhasrān rāmaḥ paraśunā tadā
07_008_0843 na hy amr̥śyata tā vāco yās tair bhr̥śam udīritāḥ
07_008_0844 bhr̥go rāmābhidhāveti yadākrandan dvijottamāḥ
07_008_0845 tataḥ kāśmīradaradān kuntikṣudrakamālavān
07_008_0846 aṅgavaṅgakaliṅgāṁś ca videhāṁs tāmraliptakān
07_008_0847 rakṣovāhān vītihotrāṁs trigartān mārtikāvatān
07_008_0848 śibīn anyāṁś ca rājanyān deśe deśe sahasraśaḥ
07_008_0849 nijaghāna śitair bāṇair jāmadagnyaḥ pratāpavān
07_008_0850 koṭīśatasahasrāṇi kṣatriyāṇāṁ sahasraśaḥ
07_008_0851 indragopakavarṇasya bandhujīvanibhasya ca
07_008_0852 rudhirasya parīvāhaiḥ pūrayitvā sarāṁsi ca
07_008_0853 sarvān aṣṭādaśa dvīpān vaśam ānīya bhārgavaḥ
07_008_0854 īje kratuśataiḥ puṇyaiḥ samāptavaradakṣiṇaiḥ
07_008_0855 vedīm aṣṭanavotsedhāṁ sauvarṇāṁ vidhinirmitām
07_008_0856 sarvaratnaśataiḥ pūrṇāṁ patākāśatamālinīm
07_008_0857 grāmyāraṇyaiḥ paśugaṇaiḥ saṁpūrṇāṁ ca mahīm imām
07_008_0858 rāmasya jāmadagnyasya pratijagrāha kaśyapaḥ
07_008_0859 tataḥ śatasahasrāṇi dvipendrān hemabhūṣaṇān
07_008_0860 nirdasyuṁ pr̥thivīṁ kr̥tvā śiṣṭeṣṭajanasaṁkulām
07_008_0861 kaśyapāya dadau rāmo hayamedhe mahāmakhe
07_008_0862 triḥsaptakr̥tvaḥ pr̥thivīṁ kr̥tvā niḥkṣatriyāṁ prabhuḥ
07_008_0863 iṣṭvā kratuśatair vīro brāhmaṇebhyo hy amanyata
07_008_0864 sa kaśyapasya vacanāt protsārya saritāṁ patim
07_008_0865 iṣupāte yudhāṁ śreṣṭhaḥ kurvan brāhmaṇaśāsanam
07_008_0866 adhyāvasad giriśreṣṭhaṁ mahendraṁ parvatottamam
07_008_0867 evaṁ guṇaśatair juṣṭo bhr̥gūṇāṁ kīrtivardhanaḥ
07_008_0868 jāmadagnyo ’py atiyaśā mariṣyati mahādyutiḥ
07_008_0869 tvayā caturbhadrataraḥ putrāt puṇyataras tava
07_008_0870 ayajvānam adākṣiṇyaṁ mā putram anutapyathāḥ
07_008_0871 ete caturbhadratarās tvayā bhadraśatādhikāḥ
07_008_0872 mr̥tā naravaraśreṣṭhā mariṣyanti ca sr̥ñjaya
07_008=0872 Colophon.
07_008=0872 vyāsa uvāca
07_008_0873 puṇyam ākhyānam āyuṣyaṁ śrutvā ṣoḍaśarājakam
07_008_0874 avyāharan narapatis tūṣṇīm āsīt sa sr̥ñjayaḥ
07_008_0875 tam abravīt tathāsīnaṁ nārado bhagavān r̥ṣiḥ
07_008_0876 śrutaṁ kīrtayato mahyaṁ gr̥hītaṁ te mahādyute
07_008_0877 āho svid antato naṣṭaṁ śrāddhaṁ śūdrāpatāv iva
07_008_0878 sa evam uktaḥ pratyāha prāñjaliḥ sr̥ñjayas tadā
07_008_0879 putraśokāpahaṁ śrutvā dhanyam ākhyānam uttamam
07_008_0880 rājarṣīṇāṁ purāṇānāṁ yajvanāṁ dakṣiṇāvatām
07_008_0881 vismayena hate śoke tamasīvārkatejasā
07_008_0882 vipāpmāsmy avyathopeto brūhi kiṁ karavāṇy aham
07_008=0882 nārada uvāca
07_008_0883 diṣṭyāpahataśokas tvaṁ vr̥ṇīṣveha yad icchasi
07_008_0884 tat tat prapatsyase sarvaṁ na mr̥ṣāvādino vayam
07_008=0884 sr̥ñjaya uvāca
07_008_0885 etenaiva pratīto ’haṁ prasanno yad bhavān mama
07_008_0886 prasanno yasya bhagavān na tasyāstīha durlabham
07_008=0886 nārada uvāca
07_008_0887 punar dadāmi te putraṁ dasyubhir nihataṁ vr̥thā
07_008_0888 uddhr̥tya narakāt kaṣṭāt paśum aprokṣitaṁ yathā
07_008=0888 vyāsa uvāca
07_008_0889 prādur āsīt tataḥ putraḥ sr̥ñjayasyādbhutaprabhaḥ
07_008_0890 prasannenarṣiṇā dattaḥ kuberatanayopamaḥ
07_008_0891 tataḥ saṁgamya putreṇa prītimān abhavan nr̥paḥ
07_008_0892 īje ca kratubhir mukhyaiḥ samāptavaradakṣiṇaiḥ
07_008_0893 akr̥tāstraś ca bhītaś ca na ca sāṁnāhiko hataḥ
07_008_0894 ayajvā cānapatyaś ca tato ’sau jīvitaḥ punaḥ
07_008_0895 śūro vīraḥ kr̥tāstraś ca pramathyārīn sahasraśaḥ
07_008_0896 abhimanyur gataḥ svargaṁ pr̥tanābhimukho hataḥ
07_008_0897 brahmacaryeṇa yān kāṁś cit prajñayā ca śrutena ca
07_008_0898 iṣṭaiś ca kratubhir yānti tāṁs te putro ’kṣayān gataḥ
07_008_0899 vidvāṁsaḥ karmabhiḥ puṇyaiḥ svargam īhanti nityaśaḥ
07_008_0900 na tu svargād ayaṁ lokaḥ kāmyate svargavāsibhiḥ
07_008_0901 tasmāt svargagataṁ putram arjunasya hataṁ raṇe
07_008_0902 nehānayituṁ śakyaṁ hi kiṁ cid aprāpyam īśitum
07_008_0903 evaṁ jñātvā sthiro bhūtvā mā śuco dhairyam āpnuhi
07_008_0904 jīvanta eva naḥ śocyā na tu svargagatānagha
07_008_0905 śocato hi mahārāja agham evābhivardhate
07_008_0906 tasmāc chokaṁ parityajya śreyase prayated budhaḥ
07_008_0907 praharṣam abhimānaṁ ca sukhaprāptiṁ ca cintayan
07_008_0908 etad āhur budhāḥ śreyo na śokaḥ śoka ucyate
07_008_0909 evaṁ vidvan samuttiṣṭha prayato bhava mā śucaḥ
07_008_0910 śrutas te saṁbhavo mr̥tyos tapāṁsy anupamāni ca
07_008_0911 sarvabhūtasamatvaṁ ca cañcalāś ca vibhūtayaḥ
07_008_0912 sr̥ñjayasya tu taṁ putraṁ mr̥taṁ saṁjīvitaṁ punaḥ
07_008_0913 evaṁ vidvan mahārāja mā śucaḥ sādhayāmy aham
07_008=0913 saṁjaya uvāca
07_008_0914 etāvad uktvā bhagavāṁs tatraivāntaradhīyata
07_008_0915 vāgīśāne bhagavati vyāse vyabhranabhaḥprabhe
07_008_0916 gate matimatāṁ śreṣṭhe samāśvāsya yudhiṣṭhiram
07_008_0917 pūrveṣāṁ pārthivendrāṇāṁ mahendrapratimaujasām
07_008_0918 nyāyādhigatavittānāṁ tāṁ śrutvā yajñasaṁpadam
07_008_0919 saṁpūjya manasā vidvān viśoko ’bhūd yudhiṣṭhiraḥ
07_008_0920 punaś cācintayad dīnaḥ kiṁ svid vakṣye dhanaṁjayam
07_008=0920 Colophon.

07_009_0002 vitrāsayantaṁ tāṁ senāṁ kaunteyānāṁ mahīpate
07_009_0003 dr̥ṣṭvā tato maheṣvāso nighnantaṁ ca rathān bhr̥śam
07_009_0004 ghaṭotkaco mahābāhū raṇāyābhijagāma ha
07_009_0005 piśācavadanair yuktaṁ rathaṁ kāñcanabhūṣitam
07_009_0006 samāsthāya mahārāja nānāpraharaṇair vr̥tam
07_009_0007 daṁśitas tapanīyena kavacena suvarcasā
07_009_0008 bhūṣaṇair ācitāṅgaś ca nadann iva ca toyadaḥ
07_009_0009 haiḍimbeyaḥ susaṁkruddho droṇam abhyadravad balī
07_009_0010 tam abhyadhāvad āyāntaṁ kruddharūpam alambusaḥ
07_009_0011 r̥kṣacarmaparikṣiptaṁ ratham āsthāya daṁśitaḥ
07_009_0012 raktoṣṭhaḥ sadhanuḥ prāṁśuḥ kalpavr̥kṣa iva sthitaḥ
07_009_0013 kṣipañ chataghnīṁ vipulāṁ musalopalatomarān
07_009_0014 musaṇṭhīr bahulāś caiva triśūlān api paṭṭasān
07_009_0015 karpaṇāñ śatadhārāṁś ca pinākān vividhāṁs tathā
07_009_0016 cakrāṇi ca kṣuraprāṇi kṣepaṇīś ca kaṭaṁkaṭān
07_009_0017 nārācān vividhān asyan sakaṅkolūkavāyasaḥ
07_009_0018 cikṣepa dhanur ādāya ninadan bhairavān ravān
07_009_0019 taṁ raudraṁ krūram āyāntaṁ dr̥ṣṭvā kālam ivāgatam
07_009_0020 prādravad bhayasaṁvignā sā rājan pāṇḍuvāhinī
07_009_0021 satyakas tu naravyāghro dr̥ṣṭvā taṁ rākṣasaṁ yudhi
07_009_0022 abhyayād amaraprakhyo bhrāmayitvā mahad dhanuḥ
07_009_0023 abhyadravac ca tad rakṣas tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti cābravīt
07_009_0024 alambusaṁ rākṣasendraṁ so ’stravarṣair avākirat
07_009_0025 tataḥ pāṇḍavasainyāni vidrutāny atha bhārata
07_009_0026 nirīkṣyābhyadravat tūrṇaṁ tvaramāṇo ghaṭotkacaḥ
07_009_0027 cikṣepa ca gadāśaktīs tomarān atha paṭṭasān
07_009_0028 hemacitratsarūn ugrān khaḍgān ākāśasaprabhān
07_009_0029 anyonyam ārād ālokya rākṣasau tau mahābalau
07_009_0030 bhairavaṁ nadatur nādān satoyāv iva toyadau
07_009_0031 tataḥ pravavr̥te yuddhaṁ ghoraṁ rākṣasasiṁhayoḥ
07_009_0032 yādr̥g eva purā vr̥ttaṁ rāmarāvaṇayor mr̥dhe
07_009_0033 tau śaktīś ca pinākāṁś ca vajrān khaḍgān paraṣvadhān
07_009_0034 anyonyam abhisaṁkruddhau tadā vyasr̥jatām ubhau
07_009_0035 ākr̥ṣyamāṇe dhanuṣī tayor bāhubalena ca
07_009_0036 yantreṇeva tadā rājan bhr̥śaṁ nādān pracakratuḥ
07_009_0037 alambusas tataś cakraṁ kr̥tāntajvalanaprabham
07_009_0038 ghaṭotkacāya cikṣepa yatnam āsthāya vīryavān
07_009_0039 tad bhaimaseniḥ saṁprekṣya cakraṁ vegavad antare
07_009_0040 gadayā tāḍayām āsa tad dīrṇaṁ śatadhābhavat
07_009_0041 tato ’gnicūrṇaiḥ sahasā cakraghātaviniḥsr̥taiḥ
07_009_0042 daṁśakair iva sā senā patadbhir bhr̥śasaṁkulā
07_009_0043 tataḥ pratihate cakre sa vīro roṣasaṁkulaḥ
07_009_0044 prāhiṇot tarasā śūlaṁ śaktīr daśaśatās tadā
07_009_0045 jvalantīr vikirantīś ca jvālāmālāḥ sahasraśaḥ
07_009_0046 yugāntolkānibhās tīkṣṇā hemadaṇḍā mahāsvanāḥ
07_009_0047 tāś cāpatantīḥ saṁprekṣya rākṣasasya ghaṭotkacaḥ
07_009_0048 ardhacandraiḥ praciccheda nārācaiḥ kaṅkapatribhiḥ
07_009_0049 tato roṣaparītāṅgaḥ pramumoca sa rākṣasaḥ
07_009_0050 śaravarṣaṁ mahāghoraṁ ghaṭotkacarathaṁ prati

% After 7.84.21ab, S ins.:
07_010_0001 ghaṭotkaco ’py asaṁbhrāntaḥ śaravarṣaṁ mahattaram
07_010_0002 alambusavadhaprepsur mumocāgnir iva jvalan
07_010_0003 alambusarathāc cogrād ghaṭotkacarathād api
07_010_0004 śarāḥ prādurbhavanti sma dvirephā iva khād diśaḥ
07_010_0005 abhracchāyeva racitā bāṇais tatra nareśvara
07_010_0006 na sma vijñāyate kiṁ cid andhakāre kr̥te śaraiḥ
07_010_0007 tata ākarṇamuktena bhallena ca ghaṭotkacaḥ
07_010_0008 alambusasya ciccheda śiro yantur mahābalaḥ
07_010_0009 tato ’parair vegavadbhiḥ kṣurais tasya ghaṭotkacaḥ
07_010_0010 akṣamīṣāṁ yugaṁ caiva ciccheda yudhi tāḍayan
07_010_0011 avaskandya rathāt tūrṇaṁ kairmīriḥ krodhamūrchitaḥ
07_010_0012 tasmin māyāmayaṁ ghoram astravarṣaṁ vavarṣa ha
07_010_0013 ghaṭotkaco ’pyāśu rathāt praskandya sa tam eva ca
07_010_0014 māyāstreṇaiva māyāstraṁ vyadhamat samare ripoḥ
07_010_0015 haiḍimbenārdyamānas tu yudhi so ’lambuso dr̥ḍham
07_010_0016 antarhito mahārāja ghaṭotkacam ayodhayat
07_010_0017 antardhānagataṁ dr̥ṣṭvā tatra tatra ghaṭotkacaḥ
07_010_0018 gadayā tāḍayām āsa vegavatyā mahābalaḥ
07_010_0019 utpapāta tato vyomni prahāraparipīḍitaḥ
07_010_0020 alambuso rākṣasendraḥ sahasā pakṣirāḍ iva
07_010_0021 ghaṭotkaco ’py asaṁbhrāntaḥ khaḍgapāṇir athotpatat
07_010_0022 tato vegena mahatā vivarṣiṣur ivāmbudaḥ
07_010_0023 tam āpatantaṁ saṁprekṣya kairmīrī rākṣasottamaḥ
07_010_0024 abhidudrāva vegena siṁhaḥ siṁham iva sthitam
07_010_0025 dakṣiṇenāsim udyamya vakṣaḥ pracchādya varmaṇā
07_010_0026 abhidudrāva vegena vegavantaṁ ghaṭotkacaḥ
07_010_0027 tāv ubhau vegasaṁrabdhāv alambusaghaṭotkacau
07_010_0028 anyonyasya tathaivorū samājaghnatur añjasā
07_010_0029 anyonyasyābhighātena tayo rākṣasasiṁhayoḥ
07_010_0030 śailenābhihatasyaiva śailasyābhūn mahāsvanaḥ
07_010_0031 tatopasr̥tya sahasā punar āpetatur bhr̥śam
07_010_0032 carantāv asimārgāṁs tān vividhān rākṣasottamau
07_010_0033 tayor gātreṣu patitāv asī bhinnau nipetatuḥ
07_010_0034 vegotsr̥ṣṭe maghavatā vajre śailataṭeṣv iva
07_010_0035 tataḥ sainyāni dadr̥śus tad yuddham atidāruṇam
07_010_0036 yuddhaṁ tayo rākṣasayor āmiṣe śyenayor iva
07_010_0037 tato lohitaraktākṣāv ubhau tau rākṣasottamau
07_010_0038 adr̥śyetāṁ tu śārdūlau sandhyāraktāv ivāmbudau
07_010_0039 cakrāte śyenavac caiva maṇḍalāni sahasraśaḥ
07_010_0040 ubhau nistriṁśahastau tau sapakṣāv iva pakṣiṇau
07_010_0041 tato bhrāmya tu taṁ khaḍgaṁ pāṇḍoḥ kirmīranandanaḥ
07_010_0042 cikṣepāsya śiro hartuṁ sa ca tasya ghaṭotkacaḥ
07_010_0043 tāv asī yugapad dīptau sametya vipulau bhuvi
07_010_0044 patitau tau tu bāhubhyāṁ rākṣasau samasajjatām
07_010_0045 śīrṣaghātāṁsaghātaiś ca parasparam athāhatau
07_010_0046 punar vimiśritau vīrau vyāyudhyete muhur muhuḥ

% After 7.118.15, Dn2 D2.3.5.6 S ins.:
07_013=0000 saṁjaya uvāca
07_013_0001 evam ukto raṇe pārtho bhūriśravasam abravīt
07_013_0002 vyaktam eva hi jīrṇaḥ san buddhiṁ rañjayase nr̥pa
07_013_0003 anarthakam idaṁ sarvaṁ yat tvayā vyāhr̥taṁ prabho
07_013_0004 hr̥ṣīkeśena saṁbandhaṁ garhase māṁ ca mūḍhavat
07_013_0005 raṇānām asi dharmajñaḥ sarvaśāstrārthapāragaḥ
07_013_0006 na cādharmam ahaṁ kuryāṁ jānaṁś caiva hi muhyase
07_013_0007 yudhyante kṣatriyāḥ śatrūn svaiḥ svaiḥ parivr̥tā nr̥pa
07_013_0008 bhrātr̥bhiḥ pitr̥bhiḥ putrais tathā saṁbandhibāndhavaiḥ
07_013_0009 vayasyair atha mitraiś ca svabāhubalam āśritāḥ
07_013_0010 ahaṁ hi sātyakiṁ śiṣyaṁ sukhasaṁbandhim eva ca
07_013_0011 madarthe yudhyamānaṁ ca tyaktvā prāṇān sudustyajān
07_013_0012 mama bāhuṁ raṇe rājan dakṣiṇaṁ yuddhadurmadam
07_013_0013 nikr̥tyamānaṁ taṁ dr̥ṣṭvā kathaṁ śatruvaśaṁ gatam
07_013_0014 tvayā nikr̥ṣyamāṇaṁ ca dr̥ṣṭavān asmi niṣkriyam
07_013_0015 na cātmā rakṣitavyo hi eko raṇagatena hi
07_013_0016 yo yasya yudhyate ’rthāya sa saṁrakṣyo narādhipa
07_013_0017 tai rakṣyamāṇaḥ sa nr̥po rakṣitavyo mahāmr̥dhe
07_013_0018 yady ahaṁ sātyakiṁ dr̥ṣṭvā tūṣṇīm āsiṣya āhave
07_013_0019 tatas tena viyogaś ca prāpyaṁ narakam eva ca
07_013_0020 rakṣitaś ca mayā yasmāt tasmāl labdho mayā sa ca
07_013_0021 yaśaś caiva svapakṣebhyaḥ phalaṁ mitrasya rakṣaṇāt
07_013_0022 yac ca māṁ garhase rājann anyena saha saṁgatam
07_013_0023 kas tena saṁgamaṁ necchet tatra te buddhivibhramaḥ
07_013_0024 kavacaṁ dhunvatas tubhyaṁ rathaṁ cārohataḥ svayam
07_013_0025 dhanurjyāṁ karṣataś caiva yudhyataḥ saha śatrubhiḥ
07_013_0026 evaṁ rathagajākīrṇe hayapattisamākule
07_013_0027 siṁhanādoddhatarave gambhīre sainyasāgare
07_013_0028 svaiś cāpi samupetasya vikrāntasya tathā raṇe
07_013_0029 satyakena kathaṁ yogyaḥ saṁgrāmas te bhaviṣyati
07_013_0030 bahubhiḥ saha saṁgamya nirjitya ca mahārathān
07_013_0031 śrāntaś ca śrāntavāhaś ca kṣīṇasarvāyudhas tvayā
07_013_0032 īdr̥śaṁ sātyakiṁ saṁkhye nirjitya ca mahāratham
07_013_0033 adhikaṁ tvaṁ vijānīṣe tathāpy anyamanā bhavān
07_013_0034 yad icchasi śiraś cāsya asinā hartum āhave
07_013_0035 tathā kr̥cchragataṁ dr̥ṣṭvā sātyakiṁ kaḥ kṣamiṣyati
07_013_0036 tvaṁ tu garhaya cātmānam ātmānaṁ yo na rakṣasi
07_013_0037 kathaṁ kariṣyase vīra yo vā tvāṁ saṁśrayej janaḥ
07_013_0038 āttaśastrasya hi raṇe vr̥ṣṇīputraṁ jighāṁsataḥ
07_013_0039 chinnavān yad ahaṁ bāhuṁ naital lokavigarhitam
07_013_0040 nyastaśastrasya hi punar vikalasya vivarmaṇaḥ
07_013_0041 abhimanyor vadhaṁ tāta dhārmikaḥ ko nu pūjayet

07_020_0027 dahantam ahitān sainye nainaṁ kaś cid avārayat
07_020_0028 yo yo hi pramukhe tasya tasthau droṇasya pūruṣaḥ
07_020_0029 tasya tasya śiraś chittvā yayau droṇaśaraḥ kṣitim
07_020_0030 evaṁ sā pāṇḍavī senā vadhyamānā mahātmanā
07_020_0031 pradudrāva punar bhītā paśyataḥ savyasācinaḥ
07_020_0032 saṁprabhagnaṁ balaṁ dr̥ṣṭvā droṇena niśi bhārata
07_020_0033 govindam abravīj jiṣṇur gaccha droṇarathaṁ prati
07_020_0034 tato rajatagokṣīrakundendusadr̥śaprabhān
07_020_0035 codayām āsa dāśārho hayān droṇarathaṁ prati
07_020_0036 bhīmaseno ’pi taṁ dr̥ṣṭvā yāntaṁ droṇāya phalgunam
07_020_0037 svasārathim uvācedaṁ droṇānīkāya māṁ vaha
07_020_0038 so ’pi tasya vacaḥ śrutvā viśoko vāhayad dhayān
07_020_0039 pr̥ṣṭhataḥ satyasandhasya jiṣṇor bharatasattama
07_020_0040 tau dr̥ṣṭvā bhrātarau yattau droṇānīkam abhidrutau
07_020_0041 pāñcālāḥ sr̥ñjayā matsyāś cedikārūṣakośalāḥ
07_020_0042 anvagacchan mahārāja kekayāś ca mahārathāḥ
07_020_0043 tato rājann abhūd ghoraḥ saṁgrāmo lomaharṣaṇaḥ
07_020_0044 bībhatsur dakṣiṇaṁ pārśvam uttaraṁ ca vr̥kodaraḥ
07_020_0045 mahadbhyāṁ rathavr̥ndābhyāṁ balaṁ jagr̥hatus tava
07_020_0046 tau dr̥ṣṭvā puruṣavyāghrau bhīmasenadhanaṁjayau
07_020_0047 dhr̥ṣṭadyumno ’bhyayād rājan sātyakiś ca mahābalaḥ
07_020_0048 caṇḍavātābhipannānām udadhīnām iva svanaḥ
07_020_0049 āsīd rājan balaughānāṁ tadānyonyam abhighnatām
07_020_0050 saumadattivadhāt kruddho dr̥ṣṭvā sātyakim āhave

07_024_0051 te tu drauṇidhanurmuktāḥ śarā hemavibhūṣitāḥ
07_024_0052 ajasram anvakīryanta ghorā bhīmarathaṁ prati
07_024_0053 tatrādbhutam apaśyāma bhīmasenasya vikramam
07_024_0054 balaṁ vīryaṁ prabhāvaṁ ca vyavasāyaṁ ca bhārata
07_024_0055 tāṁ sa meghād ivodbhūtāṁ bāṇavr̥ṣṭiṁ samantataḥ
07_024_0056 jalavr̥ṣṭiṁ mahāghorāṁ tapānta iva cintayan
07_024_0057 droṇaputravadhaprepsur bhīmo bhīmaparākramaḥ
07_024_0058 amuñcac charavarṣāṇi prāvr̥ṣīva balāhakaḥ
07_024_0059 tad rukmapr̥ṣṭhaṁ bhīmasya dhanur ghoraṁ mahad raṇe
07_024_0060 vikr̥ṣyamāṇaṁ vibabhau śakracāpam ivāparam
07_024_0061 tasmāc charāḥ prādur āsañ śataśo ’tha sahasraśaḥ
07_024_0062 saṁchādayantaḥ samare drauṇim āhavaśobhinam
07_024_0063 tayor visr̥jator evaṁ śarajālāni māriṣa
07_024_0064 vāyur apyantarā rājan nāśaknot pratisarpitum
07_024_0065 tathā drauṇir mahārāja śarān hemavibhūṣitān
07_024_0066 tailadhautān prasannāgrān prāhiṇod vadhakāṅkṣayā
07_024_0067 tān antarikṣe viśikhais tridhaikaikam aśātayat
07_024_0068 viśeṣayan droṇasutaṁ tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti cābravīt
07_024_0069 punaś ca śaravarṣāṇi ghorāṇy ugrāṇi pāṇḍavaḥ
07_024_0070 vyasr̥jad balavān kruddho droṇaputravadhepsayā
07_024_0071 tato ’stramāyayā tūrṇaṁ śaravr̥ṣṭiṁ nivārya tām
07_024_0072 dhanuś ciccheda bhīmasya droṇaputro mahāstravit
07_024_0073 śaraiś cainaṁ subahubhiḥ kruddhaḥ saṁkhye parābhinat
07_024_0074 sa chinnadhanvā balavān rathaśaktiṁ sudāruṇām
07_024_0075 vegenāvidhya cikṣepa droṇaputrarathaṁ prati
07_024_0076 tām āpatantīṁ sahasā maholkābhāṁ śitaiḥ śaraiḥ
07_024_0077 ciccheda samare drauṇir darśayan pāṇilāghavam
07_024_0078 etasminn antare bhīmo dr̥ḍham ādāya kārmukam
07_024_0079 drauṇiṁ vivyādha viśikhaiḥ smayamāno vr̥kodaraḥ
07_024_0080 tato drauṇir mahārāja bhīmasenasya sārathim
07_024_0081 lalāṭe dārayām āsa śareṇānataparvaṇā
07_024_0082 so ’tividdho balavatā droṇaputreṇa sārathiḥ
07_024_0083 vyāmoham agamad rājan raśmīn utsr̥jya vājinām
07_024_0084 tato ’śvāḥ prādravaṁs tūrṇaṁ mohite rathasārathau
07_024_0085 bhīmasenasya rājendra paśyatāṁ sarvadhanvinām
07_024_0086 taṁ dr̥ṣṭvā pradrutair aśvair apakr̥ṣṭaṁ raṇājirāt
